# Parity ledger

Generated by `node scripts/ledger/build.mjs`; do not edit rows by hand except `gpui`, `status` and `evidence` (kept across rebuilds, matched by id).
Format follows port.md §3. `status`: todo / done / accepted-difference (only the user sets the last). `scenes`: harness scenes that verify the row (a trailing `?` = not in the harness yet).

Id scheme: `C.<css file>.<selector slug>.<hash of @-context+selector>.<property>` (CSS, one row per declaration), `T.<tsx file>.<function>.<kind>.<hash of the expression text>` (TSX), `S.<key>` (DEFAULTS entry), `I.<id>` (Settings item), `W.<kind>.<hash>` (parsed main.cjs), `H.<key>` (curated). A repeat of an id gets `~2`, `~3`.
